# The Smoke Control Areas (Exempted Fireplaces) (England) (No. 2) Order 2014

Made: 2nd September 2014

Laid before Parliament: 9th September 2014

Coming into force: 1st October 2014

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Citation, application and commencement

###### 1

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Fireplaces exempted from the provisions of section 20 of the Clean Air Act 1993

###### 2

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Revocation

###### 3

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
